CMPXCHG8B_LOCK (M64) - Latency

Operands

  • Operand 1 (r/w): Memory
  • Operand 2 (r/w, suppressed): Register (EDX)
  • Operand 3 (r/w, suppressed): Register (EAX)
  • Operand 4 (r, suppressed): Register (ECX)
  • Operand 5 (r, suppressed): Register (EBX)
  • Operand 6 (w, suppressed): Flags (ZF: w)

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤47

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, index register): ≤14

Latency operand 1 → 1 (memory): 10

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, base register): 9

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, index register): 8

Latency operand 1 → 2 (memory): ≤10

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, base register): 9

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, index register): 8

Latency operand 1 → 3 (memory): ≤10
